# 004C1BR — Stage B virtual installed-state derivation qualification

Status: `QUALIFICATION_CANDIDATE / STATIC_DETERMINISTIC_STAGE_B_VIRTUAL_STATE_DERIVATION_ONLY / ZERO_SOLVER_EXECUTION`
Issue: #7
Canonical base: `b740e403b60e12b88aa9dd3e659b2565c1071b39`
Canonical base tree: `7fd26a0b2b419fb32baaa7c964799f21d9d11b54`
Authority: `github:issue-comment:5626211557`

## 1. Purpose and authority boundary

004C1BR derives the solver-model-only installed state that a later Stage C simulation would consume. It applies the exact byte-identical canonical Stage B transaction established by Replay A/B to the exact 004C1BJ virtual Stage A predecessor state. It does not execute Docker, APT, apt-config, dpkg, package actions, Stage C, PDFium, or any provider runtime.

```text
004C1BR_AUTHORITY = STATIC_DETERMINISTIC_STAGE_B_VIRTUAL_STATE_DERIVATION_ONLY
004C1BR_ALLOWED_REPOSITORY_PATH = specs/004-local-pdf-core/004c1br-stage-b-virtual-installed-state-derivation-qualification.md
004C1BR_MAX_CHANGED_REPOSITORY_FILES = 1
DOCKER_CONTAINER_EXECUTION = 0
APT_GET_EXECUTION = 0
APT_CONFIG_EXECUTION = 0
DPKG_EXECUTION = 0
PACKAGE_DOWNLOAD_INSTALL_UNPACK_CONFIGURE = 0
STAGE_B_REPLAY_RETRY = 0
STAGE_C_SOLVER_EXECUTION = NOT_AUTHORIZED
PDFIUM_OR_PROVIDER_EXECUTION = NOT_AUTHORIZED
004C2 = NOT_AUTHORIZED
004D = NOT_AUTHORIZED
SPECIFICATION_005 = NOT_AUTHORIZED
```

The derived state is a solver model only. It makes no claim that package payloads were unpacked, maintainer scripts or triggers ran, alternatives/diversions changed, services changed, or a real provisioned filesystem exists.

Replay A and Replay B produced byte-identical canonical Stage B transaction JSONL and selected-archive identity sets. 004C1BP separately accepts exactly the sole predeclared conflict transition; the parser findings themselves remain preserved.

## 3. Deterministic transition semantics

004C1BR follows the canonical 004C1BJ state-construction pattern and extends it only for the exact Stage B action set:

1. Verify predecessor dpkg status, predecessor installed-state JSON, Stage B transaction, and Stage B input transport by exact byte length and SHA-256.
2. Parse the predecessor status into exactly 255 unique `(Package, Architecture)` paragraphs and require exact equality with the canonical predecessor installed-state JSON.
3. Require exactly 718 Stage B records with action cardinality `650 INSTALL / 59 UPGRADE / 1 REMOVE / 8 UNCHANGED_REQUESTED_ROOT` and no other action.
4. For every `INSTALL` or `UPGRADE`, select exactly one stanza from the exact snapshot index named by the canonical transaction and require exact package/version/architecture, `Filename`, `Size`, and `SHA256` equality. Insert exactly `Status: install ok installed` immediately after the unchanged first `Package:` line.
5. For `UPGRADE`, require the predecessor key and exact `fromVersion`, then replace that paragraph in its predecessor position.
6. For `INSTALL`, require the key to be absent from the predecessor and from prior installs, then append new paragraphs sorted by `(package, architecture)`.
7. For `REMOVE`, accept exactly one identity only: `pkg-config:amd64@0.29.2-1ubuntu3`. Require the exact predecessor key/version and remove that paragraph. Any other removal fails closed.
8. For `UNCHANGED_REQUESTED_ROOT`, require the predecessor key to exist and `fromVersion == toVersion == predecessor Version`; emit no state mutation.
9. Serialize surviving/replaced predecessor paragraphs in predecessor order, followed by lexicographically sorted new installs, with exactly one blank line between paragraphs and one final blank line.
10. Independently derive canonical installed-state JSON from the output status, sorted by `(package, architecture, version)`, containing only `package`, `architecture`, `status`, and `version`.
11. Require exactly 904 unique installed `(package, architecture)` keys. This cardinality is mechanically `255 + 650 INSTALL - 1 REMOVE = 904`; upgrades and unchanged-root records do not change key count.
12. Emit a canonical transition manifest binding every mutated or checked record to predecessor/source/virtual stanza identities as applicable.

The `REMOVE` transition remains the exact named policy disposition already qualified by 004C1BO/004C1BP; this derivation does not introduce generic removal acceptance.

## 4. Frozen transformer

```text
TRANSFORMER_BYTES = 10985
TRANSFORMER_SHA256 = 1a40578663ca32c8424c0f13028101488d3e49c46e27759ac9d76353c8296fd8
TRANSFORMER_TRAILING_LF = TRUE
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
import argparse, hashlib, json, tarfile
from pathlib import Path

PRE_STATUS=(255642,'92b3ce89044000c132ac935dd8677f9306f112c14fba45a500b7af8c8edf3263')
PRE_STATE=(27625,'14d0da8d33b804702e4b23fa0a8ee99da33feb32641afe5c8e8649504f731ae7')
STAGE_B_TX=(357404,'eb4851027ba426113da526f23a6f1b6edcf12a832ab6c9df53c767c2656ec317')
TRANSPORT=(204800000,'bcc764555216562839fe10ef8478832f44cad027ef284c97d8edba65e28d4a72')
EXPECTED={'INSTALL':650,'UPGRADE':59,'REMOVE':1,'UNCHANGED_REQUESTED_ROOT':8}
EXPECTED_OUTPUT_COUNT=904
EXPECTED_REMOVE=('pkg-config','amd64','0.29.2-1ubuntu3')
INDEXES={
 ('jammy-security','main'):(20025980,'dbd457849762832bbe31a16ebf4bd9589ee199c098dfab9644ff492e93104f91'),
 ('jammy-security','multiverse'):(531283,'0d2c5a73d67917c70c6aceb3cb8cf6cad539e7b7c9a89a1ff83b48264cf95acf'),
 ('jammy-security','restricted'):(36809356,'8a8b903ffb5de341a37b3bdf202bf519a7ab0d17c4c1ff66eb8c19363f807ae5'),
 ('jammy-security','universe'):(6183008,'3230ca3f52e3afccff85b059a60019aa4be26342ca8cdfdda04e0e50f5ac7fbc'),
 ('jammy-updates','main'):(21442327,'0009b768364a862e7754f782f3f0dda9730f938f300179c86f17e39ba12ffc0b'),
 ('jammy-updates','multiverse'):(568152,'848df514867b80a7fcbbf0453f7a76eb8e29688b094707e52981c2a7d80722db'),
 ('jammy-updates','restricted'):(38216851,'68a38a898e28d680b094ef435c212d9664e8441acf01ea652916471f9455b479'),
 ('jammy-updates','universe'):(7269024,'1679f3823d2077dc6e7d72e16494a33b03c2fbba26b806423af3df05c60eed7b'),
 ('jammy','main'):(6779186,'12ce0797a6ed39a1fee1321985976c6b78872a8fe4c7ef2fb712f2364c9839a3'),
 ('jammy','multiverse'):(918002,'3493e180d952143ec601e4c833e54de709473bd636b6a3f6bb94e367e56f4abf'),
 ('jammy','restricted'):(915647,'b40bcc8340f4a2295e3daa158b7c50001642e843b3559907402c1b0da2d2459b'),
 ('jammy','universe'):(64332414,'6b8cc68643d18250ab297c4f6d427a8778b1d1534e1a3033fff0f221fa20a419')}

def sha(b): return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def ident(b): return len(b),sha(b)
def require_file(path,expected,label):
 b=Path(path).read_bytes()
 if ident(b)!=expected: raise RuntimeError(f'{label} identity mismatch: {ident(b)}')
 return b

def paragraphs(data,label):
 if not data.endswith(b'\n\n'): raise RuntimeError(f'{label} missing final blank line')
 out=data[:-2].split(b'\n\n') if data[:-2] else []
 if any(not p for p in out): raise RuntimeError(f'{label} empty paragraph')
 return out

def fields(stanza,label):
 out={}; current=None
 for line in stanza.split(b'\n'):
  if line[:1] in (b' ',b'\t'):
   if current is None: raise RuntimeError(f'{label} orphan continuation')
   continue
  if b':' not in line: raise RuntimeError(f'{label} malformed field')
  k,v=line.split(b':',1); key=k.decode('ascii')
  if key in out: raise RuntimeError(f'{label} duplicate field {key}')
  out[key]=(v[1:] if v.startswith(b' ') else v).decode('utf-8'); current=key
 return out

def cjson(v): return (json.dumps(v,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'),sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode()
def member(s,c): return f'tmp/signthos-apt/lists/snapshot.ubuntu.com_ubuntu_20260909T180000Z_dists_{s}_{c}_binary-amd64_Packages'

def load_indexes(path):
 require_file(path,TRANSPORT,'Stage B transport'); maps={}
 with tarfile.open(path,'r:') as tf:
  st=tf.extractfile('tmp/signthos-apt/state/status').read()
  if ident(st)!=PRE_STATUS: raise RuntimeError('transport predecessor status mismatch')
  for sc,expected in INDEXES.items():
   data=tf.extractfile(member(*sc)).read()
   if ident(data)!=expected: raise RuntimeError(f'index identity mismatch: {sc}')
   mp={}
   for i,stanza in enumerate(paragraphs(data,f'index {sc}')):
    f=fields(stanza,f'index {sc}[{i}]')
    if not all(x in f for x in ('Package','Version','Architecture')): continue
    mp.setdefault((f['Package'],f['Version'],f['Architecture']),[]).append((stanza,f))
   maps[sc]=mp
 return maps

def transform(pre_status,pre_state,transaction,index_maps):
 paras=paragraphs(pre_status,'predecessor status')
 pf=[fields(p,f'predecessor[{i}]') for i,p in enumerate(paras)]; keys={}
 for i,f in enumerate(pf):
  for need in ('Package','Architecture','Version','Status'):
   if need not in f: raise RuntimeError(f'predecessor[{i}] missing {need}')
  k=(f['Package'],f['Architecture'])
  if k in keys: raise RuntimeError(f'duplicate predecessor key {k}')
  keys[k]=i
 derived=sorted(({'package':f['Package'],'architecture':f['Architecture'],'status':f['Status'],'version':f['Version']} for f in pf),key=lambda r:(r['package'],r['architecture'],r['version']))
 expected_state=json.loads(pre_state)
 if derived!=sorted(expected_state,key=lambda r:(r['package'],r['architecture'],r['version'])): raise RuntimeError('predecessor state mismatch')
 recs=[json.loads(x) for x in transaction.decode().splitlines() if x]
 counts={a:sum(r.get('action')==a for r in recs) for a in EXPECTED}
 if len(recs)!=718 or counts!=EXPECTED: raise RuntimeError(f'transaction cardinality mismatch: {len(recs)} {counts}')
 upgrades={}; installs=[]; install_keys=set(); removes=set(); unchanged=set(); manifest=[]
 for n,r in enumerate(recs):
  if r.get('stageId')!='STAGE_B' or r.get('action') not in EXPECTED: raise RuntimeError(f'unsupported record {n}')
  act=r['action']; key=(r.get('package'),r.get('architecture'))
  if None in key: raise RuntimeError(f'missing package key at record {n}')
  if act=='REMOVE':
   if (r.get('package'),r.get('architecture'),r.get('fromVersion'))!=EXPECTED_REMOVE: raise RuntimeError('unqualified REMOVE identity')
   if any(r.get(x) is not None for x in ('toVersion','filename','archiveBytes','archiveSha256','canonicalPackageMetadataSha256','selectedSuite','selectedComponent','reasonClass')): raise RuntimeError('REMOVE carries selected metadata')
   if key not in keys or pf[keys[key]]['Version']!=r['fromVersion']: raise RuntimeError('REMOVE predecessor mismatch')
   if key in removes: raise RuntimeError('duplicate REMOVE')
   removes.add(key); p=paras[keys[key]]
   manifest.append({'action':act,'package':key[0],'architecture':key[1],'fromVersion':r['fromVersion'],'toVersion':None,'predecessorStanzaBytes':len(p),'predecessorStanzaSha256':sha(p)})
   continue
  if act=='UNCHANGED_REQUESTED_ROOT':
   if r.get('fromVersion') is None or r.get('toVersion')!=r.get('fromVersion'): raise RuntimeError('UNCHANGED version mismatch')
   if any(r.get(x) is not None for x in ('filename','archiveBytes','archiveSha256')): raise RuntimeError('UNCHANGED carries archive')
   if key not in keys or pf[keys[key]]['Version']!=r['fromVersion']: raise RuntimeError('UNCHANGED predecessor mismatch')
   if key in unchanged: raise RuntimeError('duplicate UNCHANGED')
   unchanged.add(key); p=paras[keys[key]]
   manifest.append({'action':act,'package':key[0],'architecture':key[1],'fromVersion':r['fromVersion'],'toVersion':r['toVersion'],'predecessorStanzaBytes':len(p),'predecessorStanzaSha256':sha(p)})
   continue
  for need in ('toVersion','filename','archiveBytes','archiveSha256','selectedSuite','selectedComponent'):
   if r.get(need) is None: raise RuntimeError(f'{act} missing {need} at {n}')
  sc=(r['selectedSuite'],r['selectedComponent']); hits=index_maps.get(sc,{}).get((r['package'],r['toVersion'],r['architecture']),[])
  if len(hits)!=1: raise RuntimeError(f'non-unique Packages stanza at {n}: {len(hits)}')
  source,sf=hits[0]
  for fld,exp in (('Filename',r['filename']),('Size',str(r['archiveBytes'])),('SHA256',r['archiveSha256'])):
   if sf.get(fld)!=exp: raise RuntimeError(f'{fld} mismatch at {n}')
  if 'Status' in sf: raise RuntimeError(f'source stanza has Status at {n}')
  lines=source.split(b'\n')
  if not lines or lines[0]!=f"Package: {r['package']}".encode(): raise RuntimeError(f'Package field not first at {n}')
  virt=b'\n'.join((lines[0],b'Status: install ok installed',*lines[1:]))
  if act=='INSTALL':
   if key in keys or key in install_keys: raise RuntimeError(f'INSTALL predecessor/duplicate at {n}')
   install_keys.add(key); installs.append((key,virt))
  else:
   if key not in keys or pf[keys[key]]['Version']!=r.get('fromVersion'): raise RuntimeError(f'UPGRADE predecessor mismatch at {n}')
   if key in upgrades: raise RuntimeError(f'duplicate UPGRADE at {n}')
   upgrades[key]=virt
  manifest.append({'action':act,'package':key[0],'architecture':key[1],'fromVersion':r.get('fromVersion'),'toVersion':r['toVersion'],'sourceIndex':f'{sc[0]}__{sc[1]}__Packages','sourceStanzaBytes':len(source),'sourceStanzaSha256':sha(source),'virtualStanzaBytes':len(virt),'virtualStanzaSha256':sha(virt)})
 if (len(installs),len(upgrades),len(removes),len(unchanged))!=(650,59,1,8): raise RuntimeError('action-set mismatch')
 out=[]
 for p,f in zip(paras,pf):
  k=(f['Package'],f['Architecture'])
  if k in removes: continue
  out.append(upgrades.get(k,p))
 out.extend(v for k,v in sorted(installs,key=lambda kv:kv[0]))
 status=b'\n\n'.join(out)+b'\n\n'
 ofs=[fields(p,f'output[{i}]') for i,p in enumerate(paragraphs(status,'output status'))]
 state=sorted(({'package':f['Package'],'architecture':f['Architecture'],'status':f['Status'],'version':f['Version']} for f in ofs),key=lambda r:(r['package'],r['architecture'],r['version']))
 if len(state)!=EXPECTED_OUTPUT_COUNT or len({(r['package'],r['architecture']) for r in state})!=EXPECTED_OUTPUT_COUNT: raise RuntimeError('output count/key mismatch')
 state_b=cjson(state)
 m={'schema':'signthos.004c1br.virtual-state-transition-manifest.v1','predecessorStatus':{'bytes':len(pre_status),'sha256':sha(pre_status)},'predecessorState':{'bytes':len(pre_state),'sha256':sha(pre_state),'count':255},'stageBTransaction':{'bytes':len(transaction),'sha256':sha(transaction),'records':718},'installCount':650,'upgradeCount':59,'removeCount':1,'unchangedRequestedRootCount':8,'qualifiedRemoval':{'package':'pkg-config','architecture':'amd64','fromVersion':'0.29.2-1ubuntu3','replacementPackage':'pkgconf','replacementArchitecture':'amd64','replacementVersion':'1.8.0-1'},'outputStatus':{'bytes':len(status),'sha256':sha(status)},'outputState':{'bytes':len(state_b),'sha256':sha(state_b),'count':len(state)},'records':manifest}
 return status,state_b,cjson(m)

def main():
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 ap.add_argument('pre_status'); ap.add_argument('pre_state'); ap.add_argument('transaction'); ap.add_argument('transport')
 ap.add_argument('out_status'); ap.add_argument('out_state'); ap.add_argument('out_manifest')
 a=ap.parse_args()
 ps=require_file(a.pre_status,PRE_STATUS,'predecessor status')
 pst=require_file(a.pre_state,PRE_STATE,'predecessor state')
 tx=require_file(a.transaction,STAGE_B_TX,'Stage B transaction')
 maps=load_indexes(a.transport)
 status,state,manifest=transform(ps,pst,tx,maps)
 Path(a.out_status).write_bytes(status); Path(a.out_state).write_bytes(state); Path(a.out_manifest).write_bytes(manifest)
 print(json.dumps({'status':{'bytes':len(status),'sha256':sha(status)},'state':{'bytes':len(state),'sha256':sha(state)},'manifest':{'bytes':len(manifest),'sha256':sha(manifest)}},sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':')))

if __name__=='__main__': main()
```

## 6. Retained deterministic output bundle

The three derived artifacts are retained below in a deterministic normalized USTAR compressed with XZ. USTAR member order is lexicographic; all members are regular files mode `0644`, uid/gid `0`, uname/gname empty, and mtime `0`.

## 7. Stage C predecessor bindings and nonclaims

If a later separately authorized Stage C harness-freeze unit is approved, it must bind exactly:

```text
STAGE_C_PREDECESSOR_STAGE_STATE_SHA256 = 42d5059095be4a8cff9661702803ce17a1b504e0fd425231cd746ae928e608ea
STAGE_C_VIRTUAL_DPKG_STATUS_SHA256 = ec5039354992576d69fa3529257684d759116385bf958cad36e1adec9a3fe7d3
STAGE_C_PREDECESSOR_INSTALLED_PACKAGE_COUNT = 904
STAGE_C_ROOT_COUNT = 4
STAGE_C_ROOTS = [curl, build-essential, pkg-config, rsync]
STAGE_C_RECOMMENDS_POLICY = NO_INSTALL_RECOMMENDS
```

004C1BR does not authorize that harness freeze or any Stage C execution. The repeated Stage C `pkg-config` root remains explicit and has not been deduplicated or rewritten.

```text
004C1BR_RESULT = PASS_STATIC_DERIVATION_CANDIDATE
REAL_PACKAGE_FILESYSTEM_EFFECTS = NOT_EXECUTED / NOT_ESTABLISHED
MAINTAINER_SCRIPT_EFFECTS = NOT_EXECUTED / NOT_ESTABLISHED
DPKG_TRIGGER_EFFECTS = NOT_EXECUTED / NOT_ESTABLISHED
ALTERNATIVES_OR_DIVERSION_EFFECTS = NOT_EXECUTED / NOT_ESTABLISHED
FINAL_PROVISIONED_IMAGE_IDENTITY = NOT_ESTABLISHED
STAGE_C_EXECUTION = NOT_AUTHORIZED
PROJECT_COMPLETE = FALSE
```
